That opened up Gerry to an attack from Kyle's [Soul Snuffers](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Soul+Snuffers), which traded with Gerry's [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter).

Kyle cast a kitchen Finks and, at the end of Kyle's turn, Gerry retraced his [Oona's Grace](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%27s+Grace). With Gerry tapped out, Kyle took the opportunity to cast [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command), bouncing Gerry's [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo) and drawing a card.

Gerry untapped and cast [Austere Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Austere+Command) to kill all creatures. Kyle responded by using Oona's ability for 5, hoping to deck Gerry if the game went long, and then allowed the [Austere Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Austere+Command) to resolve. His [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) came back as a 2/1.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/64/c9/64c9967a31e0630d0030d1cc93a5496f6eef6758156803d648c05d6340f15401.jpg)*Kyle's focus can't be shaken.*Kyle attacked with his Finks and then cast another [Oona, Queen of the Fae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%2C+Queen+of+the+Fae). Gerry countered the gigantic flier with [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command), but Kyle still had enough mana left over to cast a [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command) for 5, leaving Gerry on 7 life and returning yet another [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) to play.

Gerry recast his [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo) naming [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command) and passed the turn to Kyle. Bundgaard attacked with his [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) and [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks), leaving Gerry on 3 life. After combat Kyle cast another Finks and at the end of Kyle's turn Gerry retraced his [Oona's Grace](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%27s+Grace). After counting both players' libraries, he then retraced his [Oona's Grace](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%27s+Grace) a second time. A Thompson [Firespout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Firespout) left Kyle with only a 2/1 Finks.

Bundgaard didn't hesitate to get in with the 2/1, leaving Gerry with only a single life point. After combat Kyle played a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) that Gerry responded to by retracing [Oona's Grace](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%27s+Grace); the [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) resolved.

When Gerry tapped out to play a [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin), Bundgaard responded with a Mannequin of his own targeting [Oona, Queen of the Fae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%2C+Queen+of+the+Fae). When Thompson couldn't find an answer to the 5/5 or the [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), the players moved to Game 2.

Bundgaard 1, Thompson 0

Kyle made the first play of the second game with a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks); Gerry countered with [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions). Thompson followed the play up with a [Chameleon Colossus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chameleon+Colossus), threatening to end the game quickly with the pumpable green monster. The only response Kyle could muster was an evoked [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter). Gerry made an [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice), putting even more pressure on Kyle.

Kyle played a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), paying the full cost to keep it in play, and Gerry used a [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) to remove the blocker before knocking his opponent to 8. Kyle fired back with an evoked [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) of his own to kill Gerry's [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice), losing a [Reflecting Pool](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Reflecting+Pool) in the process. He then cast a [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin) returning [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) in an effort to buy more time.

Gerry's next attack put Kyle to 9 and left the Coloradan with a 2/1 [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks). When Gerry attacked with both of his creatures again, Kyle blocked the Colossus, falling to 6. On his next turn Kyle cast another [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) and evoked a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), but Thompson attacked with all his creatures anyway. Kyle fell to 7 and Gerry played an [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice).

Trying to climb back into things, Kyle cast a [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) returning Gerry's first Archon. The attack took Gerry to 16. With the turn back and after a bit of deliberation, Gerry attacked with his [Chameleon Colossus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chameleon+Colossus) and his [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice). Kyle chumped both of Gerry's creatures and then, with [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique)'s persist on the stack, cast [Nameless Inversion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Nameless+Inversion) to finish off Gerry's Archon. Gerry let this resolve and Kyle was able to return an [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice) with the Clique.

After combat, Gerry played [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo) naming [Oona, Queen of the Fae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%2C+Queen+of+the+Fae) and a second copy set to [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command). Kyle thought for a bit and then attacked with Archon and [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) prompting Gerry cast the last card in his hand, a [Plumeveil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plumeveil), to mug the Clique. After combat Kyle cast a [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command) killing the Archon and returning a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter). As it hit the graveyard, Kyle removed Gerry's Colossus from the game.

Gerry attacked with his [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw), putting Kyle to 4.

Kyle went to [Sower of Temptation](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sower+of+Temptation) Gerry's [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw), but Gerry had a [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) to counter it. Kyle then evoked a [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) to kill Gerry's [Plumeveil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plumeveil) and clear the way for his [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) to attack, putting Gerry to 10. Gerry attacked with his [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) putting Kyle to 1 and then cast a [Chameleon Colossus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chameleon+Colossus).

Kyle attacked with his [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) putting Gerry to 8 and then passed the turn back to Gerry.

On Gerry's turn, Kyle cast a [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) to tap down Gerry's team and draw a card. Gerry had a [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) of his own, but Kyle had yet another [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) to live to see another turn.

Kyle cast [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command) killing Gerry's [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) and returning a Finks to play and then cast a [Sower of Temptation](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sower+of+Temptation) to steal Gerry's Colossus. Thompson drew and played a [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin), returning [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) to play which killed Kyle's Sower. Wanting to stay on the offensive, Kyle attacked with his [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) and put Gerry to 6 while playing a Finks of his own to go up to 5. When Thompson attacked with [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw), Bundgaard was back to 2.

When Kyle couldn't find an answer to the fear creature, the players were off to Game 3.

Bundgaard 1, Thompson 1.

The second game started slow, with Kyle making the first play on turn six thanks to a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks). Gerry cast [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command), but Kyle had a [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions) to force through his creature. Kyle revealed a [Crib Swap](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Crib+Swap), which he put to the bottom, and Gerry revealed a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) which he kept. Gerry then played the 2/2 and passed the turn back to Kyle.

Kyle attacked with the Finks and Gerry blocked; Bundgaard had no other plays. Thompson did, however, attempting to play a [Chameleon Colossus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chameleon+Colossus), though the 4/4 ran afoul of Bundgaard's [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions). When Kyle attacked with his [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks), Gerry had a [Plumeveil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plumeveil) to once again leave Kyle with an empty board. That was quickly remedied after combat as Kyle played a [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command) for 5, returning Finks to play and putting Gerry on 15.

Gerry cast a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) and a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) making the life totals 17 for Gerry and 26 for Kyle, and Bundgaard attacked with his own Finks, which was blocked by [Plumeveil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plumeveil). Kyle had the [Nameless Inversion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Nameless+Inversion) to finish off the defender. Undeterred, Gerry made a [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) leaving Kyle with an empty board. Thompson attacked with his Finks and his [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), but Kyle had a [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin) to return his own Finks to play. The [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks)es traded.

Kyle had no plays on his turn, but Gerry had an end of turn [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin) to return a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) to play. On Thompson's next attack the 2/1 [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks)es traded again and Kyle took 7 going down to 23. After combat Gerry cast another [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter).

![](https://i.ormos.uk/62/4a/624ab0c3d5ecddb93906c8d078853a5b4993bb9412df61d28f6081c0a5e26f88.jpg)*Gerry Mulldrifters over his options.*And then another [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter).

This left Gerry with a board of four [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter)s and a [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) to combat Kyle's empty board.

Kyle cast a [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command) to kill Gerry's [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) and return a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) into play. Gerry attacked with his four [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter)s, putting Gerry to 17. After combat, Gerry cast a [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo) naming [Oona, Queen of the Fae](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oona%2C+Queen+of+the+Fae). Kyle could do nothing but attack with his Finks, putting Gerry to 16.

Gerry attacked with his four [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter)s again, putting Kyle to 9. Kyle followed suit with his Finks again, putting Gerry to 16.

Thompson attacked with the [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter)s and the only response that Kyle could muster was a [Crib Swap](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Crib+Swap) to off Gerry's Mannequined [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter). The attack put Kyle to 3 and after combat Gerry cast a Finks to put himself back up to 15.

Kyle cast a [Profane Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Profane+Command), attempting to kill a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) and by himself another turn, Gerry countered the Command with a [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions) and Kyle scooped up his cards.

Gerry Thompson defeats Kyle Bundgaard 2-1.

  

## Semifinals – Nate Elkins vs. Gerry Thompson

*by Bill Stark*

Nate Elkins had to start the first game of his semifinals match against Gerry Thompson with a mulligan. He clearly didn’t look happy about his second hand either but opted to keep the six anyway. Fortunately for him he had lost the die roll meaning he was on the draw. His opponent, Iowan Gerry Thompson, was happy with his opening hand and used a [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) evoke to kill Elkins’ first turn [Goldmeadow Stalwart](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goldmeadow+Stalwart). A [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo) then protected Thompson from [Figure of Destiny](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Figure+of+Destiny), which Elkins had played on his second and third turns.

The players were only a few turns into the match, and already Nate Elkins seemed out of things. Gerry Thompson had built up a solid manabase while his opponent had missed land drops and watched all of his early pressure negated. A [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) came down to Dismiss a [Spectral Procession](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spectral+Procession). Elkins’ deck choice, Kithkin, had been the talk of the weekend sending two players to undefeated records on the first day. It was Gerry Thompson’s Quick ‘N Toast build, however, that had been favored by most of the contingent of American pros who showed up for the event. Designed by Pat Chapin it featured new *Eventide* cards like [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo) and [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice). Unfortunately for Gerry T, Elkins had already dispatched a version of the deck in the hands of Antonino De Rosa during the quarterfinals.

That didn’t change the fact that Nate was in trouble in the semis. Gerry had managed a [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) that killed a [Goldmeadow Stalwart](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goldmeadow+Stalwart) from his opponent and had begun the beatdown, sending the score to 20-14 in Gerry’s favor. He continued keeping the counter pressure up using a [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) and [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions) to prevent back-to-back [Cloudgoat Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudgoat+Ranger)s from hitting the table. A [Wizened Cenn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wizened+Cenn) for Elkins managed to make it onto the table.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/9c/3e/9c3ecf96d8d7d8684cd95a2ec4b94fea49fa1653cfdfab95135f09058f9f7be3.jpg)*Could Gerry Thompson succeed where De Rosa failed?*Hardcasting a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) kept Thompson’s hand filled with goodness, but apparently no counters as he allowed his opponent a [Spectral Procession](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spectral+Procession). When Gerry played a [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher), everything came into focus, though the 7/7 cost him his own [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter). Still, with the totals at 22-7 in Gerry’s favor, it didn’t seem like he minded all that much. Considering he was running [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin), having the [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) in the graveyard could actually turn out to be a *good* thing.

The lack of counters in Thompson’s hand became more apparent when his opponent successfully resolved an [Ajani Goldmane](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ajani+Goldmane) with no interference from Gerry. Still, a set of [Firespout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Firespout)s cleared the way for Gerry to attack with his [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher) and he sent the 7/7 not at the planeswalker Ajani, but his opponent Nathan Elkins. When Elkins’ chump blocker the following turn met a removal spell, Nate scooped ‘em up for the second game.

Thompson 1, Elkins 0.

"Ant won Game 1 against you too, yeah?" Gerry asked his opponent, hoping to avoid a repeat of Elkins’ quarterfinals comeback.

"Yeah," Elkins nodded before taking a mulligan. "Let’s try that again."

A [Burrenton Forge-Tender](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burrenton+Forge-Tender) and [Wizened Cenn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wizened+Cenn) led the way for Elkins to start the second game while Gerry tried to play catch-up using [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks). His opponent was happy to [Crib Swap](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Crib+Swap) the 3/2, nullifying the persist ability of the Finks. With the score 20-14 in his opponent’s favor, Gerry made a [Firespout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Firespout) and, surprisingly, Elkins opted to allow it to resolve, losing his [Wizened Cenn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wizened+Cenn) and not sacrificing his Burge-Tender.

If Gerry was happy about that trade, he didn’t let on simply summoning a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) to try to swing momentum back in his favor. Elkins didn’t help those matters much making a [Spectral Procession](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spectral+Procession) to go along with his Forge-Tender and [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault). Gerry found an answer to the creature-land quickly in the form of [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo), freeing up his [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) to block the horde of 1/1 creatures staring him down. At 11 life, he seemed to have stabilized using an [Austere Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Austere+Command) to wipe his opponent’s board of all creatures.

Of course Elkins had a strong follow-up in the form of Kithkin Siege-Gang Commander... er, make that [Cloudgoat Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudgoat+Ranger). Facing an army in just one card, Gerry was content playing a second [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) and a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks), which bumped him up to 13. From there he began establishing control. [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) dealt with his opponent’s [Cloudgoat Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudgoat+Ranger) and the [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) became bold enough to attack, trading in combat with Nate’s [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault) and sending Gerry to 15 life.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/e5/a5/e5a5ed9bf43819a61d3406d2c1cde0b8b6904e1fd29b802c7d3295ee3ff3d73f.jpg)*Could Nate Elkins repeat his quarters performance?*When Elkins’ tokens started getting out of control, Gerry contentedly [Hallowed Burial](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hallowed+Burial)ed them away. When Nate tried to get ahead with [Figure of Destiny](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Figure+of+Destiny) Gerry made an [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice). When Elkins tried to rebuild with a [Spectral Procession](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spectral+Procession) and [Knight of Meadowgrain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+Meadowgrain), Gerry [Firespout](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Firespout)ed. A second [Figure of Destiny](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Figure+of+Destiny)? Gerry had a second [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo). The match was a textbook beatdown/control battle, and the spectators crowded around to see everything in detail.

Lands flew all ‘round the board to the point where the players were running out of space. The long game favored Thompson, and this one was turning into a behemoth of a battle. Thompson landed a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) to tilt the score in his favor, 17-15.

"What are the odds of me winning this game?" Elkins inquired of his opponent. Squinting his eyes in concentration Thompson responded "I’d say about 15%."

Gerry managed a [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) to blow up his opponent’s [Figure of Destiny](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Figure+of+Destiny) and put a clock on. A [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin) allowed him to return his [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice) to play, and a [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) tapping his opponent’s blockers put him in a position to attack for the win. When Elkins was informed he couldn’t untap his [Order of Whiteclay](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Order+of+Whiteclay) because it had summoning sickness in order to block, he extended his hand in defeat.

Gerry Thompson defeats Nate Elkins 2-0.

  

## Semifinal: Lee Steht vs. Kenny Castor

*by Riki Hayashi*

![](https://i.ormos.uk/e9/0a/e90a361e4ec8a6667bb43cf48e69d0ad857837161786238e15f03832ca9ede4f.jpg)

Lee went first and kept. Kenneth "you can call me Kenny" mulliganed to six.

Steht got things going quickly with a turn two [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom). Kenny’s turn two was less exciting as he missed a land. [Merrow Reejery](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merrow+Reejery) and [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) got discarded on consecutive turns, while Faerie Rogues chipped away at Kenny’s life. Lee’s [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) saw a hand with [Chameleon Colossus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chameleon+Colossus), [Mirror Entity](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mirror+Entity), Merrow Reejery, [Nameless Inversion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Nameless+Inversion), [Sage’s Dousing](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sage%E2%80%99s+Dousing), [Silvergill Adept](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Silvergill+Adept), and [Stonybrook Banneret](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stonybrook+Banneret)—a spicy hand, if Kenny could have found a second land. Lee took the Banneret, and still seeing no land on his next draw, Kenny scooped.

Steht 1, Castor 0.

Kenny gave his friend in the crowd a knowing look regarding his mulligan. "There’s no justice..." A second turn [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) prompted Lee to exclaim "What?!" to Kenny’s hand of two [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher)s, [Chameleon Colossus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chameleon+Colossus), [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) and some lands. "Thought you were playing Merfolk, man," said Lee. "Thought you were playing Faeries," deadpanned Kenny. Colossus hit the bin.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/9c/d8/9cd8b70e47a1eabac5a5b55d6d70c4b8246e907053ff8ba3801334179e3fbfd3.jpg)

Castor played a freshly drawn [Stonybrook Banneret](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stonybrook+Banneret) while Lee made [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom). Steht then went for end-of-turn [Nameless Inversion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Nameless+Inversion) on the Banneret. That met a [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) in counter-and-bounce on one of Lee’s lands. Another Nameless on Lee’s turn got the Banneret, and Kenny’s follow up Merrow Reejery died to a third Inversion. Meanwhile, [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) grew Lee a small army.

Castor addressed this problem with his two [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher)s, the first being countered.

Lee asked Kenny for a card count and followed with "Will you tell me what they are?" He did it with a dead serious face. "Thought I’d try," he shrugged to being stonewalled.

An evoked [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw) took down the second Thresher. With enough lands to have played [Shriekmaw](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shriekmaw), it was obvious to all that Lee had a counterspell. Kenny ran a [Sower of Temptation](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sower+of+Temptation) out and had the [Sage’s Dousing](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sage%E2%80%99s+Dousing) from way back when to deal with the [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command). But that was all a set up for Lee to play [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) and steal [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher) for the finish.

Lee Steht defeats Kenny Castor 2-0.

After the match, I asked Lee about his surprise upon seeing Kenny’s hand with [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) in Game 2. "Didn’t you get to see his decklist?" I asked. He had, but he was trying to break up Kenny’s stoic attitude and throw him off his game. "At this point, everyone has a good deck, so it comes down to the mental mistakes," he explained.

  

## Final: More Powerful Than a Smiley Face -Finals – Lee Steht vs. Gerry Thompson

*by Nate Price*

"MJ was supposed to win this tournament," Gerry told Antonino DeRosa as he sat next to him to watch the final match.

"How so," Ant asked puzzled?

"He won Nats," Gerry explained. "This is like the first time in three years that the Nat’s winner hasn’t pulled of the immediate GP win."

"You know you’re playing against my mentor," Ant told Gerry.

"Really?"

"Yeah, he taught me how to play **Magic**."

"I’ve been playing for a really long time," Lee explained to Gerry.

"I figure there’s a mental edge for us older players," Lee Joked. " We’ve had so much more to worry about, these games don’t matter. You kids have things like money and college to worry about."

"Wow, I don’t have any of those."

Neither player came to the table with a die to decide who would go first. Gerry grabbed a coin, but refused to flip it, stating that he "can’t ever flip coins right."

"I can’t flip it, you flip it."

"I don’t want to flip it," Lee said with a hint of confusion.

"Fine, I’ll flip it," Ant offered. After the judges agreed, the coin was in the air. When Lee called heads and it came up heads, he said play, and Gerry turned to Ant and jokingly hissed "Cheater!"

![](https://i.ormos.uk/dc/65/dc6565741484ae5c398242d32880d2a27cd9516b0f00ea15e580f255d11d95a2.jpg)*A large crowd assembled to watch the battle for first.*

Lee laid his cards out in a smiley face pattern before taking a look at them.

"A smiley face? Really," Gerry asked him almost in shock as he counted to make sure there were seven?

"Yeah, I kinda promised a judge I would."

Apparently it worked, since Lee was quite happy with his opening seven. Gerry, on the other hand, started the game off down a card on the draw. Lee had a [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) on the first turn and took a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) from a hand containing only an [Austere Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Austere+Command) and [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo). He didn’t have the requisite second-turn [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom), though, and just passed the turn back to Gerry. A [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) came down for Mr. Thompson, which would hold Lee’s [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault) at bay. When the Vault attacked on the following turn, Lee cycled through two [Peppersmoke](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Peppersmoke)s to kill the blocking Finks, leaving them tiny and out of combat.

Gerry got in for two on the following turn, but when he tried to attack on the following turn, Lee had a surprise. He activated his [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault) and blocked the Finks. Before damage went on the stack, he tried a [Scion of Oona](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scion+of+Oona), pumping his [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault) out of lethal range. Gerry had a [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions) to counter it, though, forcing Lee to blow a third [Peppersmoke](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Peppersmoke) to off the Finks. This time, they stayed dead.

Gerry finally played his [Runed Halo](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Runed+Halo), naming [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault). Lee had effects during Gerry’s next draw step, playing a [Vendilion Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vendilion+Clique) to wash a card away and get a threat that could get around the Halo. The turn after that, Lee had another effect at the beginning of Gerry’s turn, using a [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique) to tap all of his lands and champion the [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault).

Gerry was starting to drop, and hadn’t really drawn any way to get control of the game. When Lee extended into a [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom), Gerry decided to make a move. He tried to [Austere Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Austere+Command), killing all creatures, but Lee had the [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) to trump it. The ensuing attack dropped Gerry to seven. All he could manage was a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) to gain a couple life. On the next turn, after Lee had made a token from his [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom), Gerry used [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command) to tap all of his creatures and draw a card. That bought him another turn to play a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), netting himself a blocker and some cards, one of which would hopefully get him out of the situation. The first thing he found was a second [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks), bringing him up to eleven.

Lee used a [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) to shine some light on his situation. When Gerry revealed a hand consisting of two lands, he had to feel pretty good. Gerry blocked the [Vendilion Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vendilion+Clique) that came over with the rest of Lee’s team, but Lee had a [Scion of Oona](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scion+of+Oona) to knock Gerry to two. Gerry’s next draw was a blank, and he scooped it up.

"Rats."

Steht 1, Thompson 0

Before the match, Gerry was talking with Antonino about his matchup versus Faeries.

"I’ve only played it four times, and my only loss was to Paulo."

Ant mentioned that he didn’t think that Lee had lost a game all day. When I asked him, he admitted that he had somehow managed to drop one.

"Jeez," Gerry barely managed to choke out!

![](https://i.ormos.uk/f7/75/f7750fca1d3dc564ae757e842432762de8835e6b3fc98840d7cb4afd7f0a2311.jpg)*Lee Steht doesn’t lose games. Well, frequently.*

Gerry got hit by a first-turn [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) again, after which the world made so much sense to him. "No wonder you’ve only lost one game." He stole the [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions) from Gerry’s hand and left him with a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) and two [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks). When Lee played a second-turn [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom), Gerry laughed in disbelief. Things got even more scripted when Lee played a [Vendilion Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vendilion+Clique) during Gerry’s draw step, denying him a [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher).

"Mizing that one Clique is just wrecking me," Gerry said with a hand on his forehead.  
 Antonino offered an explanation. "I think he’s just playing that one clique because it’s a legend. You know, don’t wanna draw two."

Gerry had to just drop his second [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) and try to stay ahead at this point. When his fifth turn rolled around, he sent his team in at Lee. Inside Gerry’s attack, Lee played a [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique), championing a [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) token, blocking a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks), and tapping Gerry’s lands. Gerry did have a [Reflecting Pool](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Reflecting+Pool) and an evoked [Wispmare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wispmare) to kill Lee’s [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom), but that was his only play after getting Cliqued.

Lee just kept to the Faerie game plan of attacking and passing. Playing spells on your opponent’s turn is so much more rewarding than using your own. Gerry used his turn to play a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), which almost immediately jumped in front of an attacking [Vendilion Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vendilion+Clique). After combat, Lee used his [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) to draw some cards and remove Gerry’s [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) from the game which he just replaced on the following turn.

With Gerry’s hand now full of goodies, Lee [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize)d him, hoping to hit something good. What he found may have been too good. Gerry’s hand contained [Austere Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Austere+Command), [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher), [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), and [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin). Eventually, Lee settled on the [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin), but didn’t have another play. Gerry untapped and played his other [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), bringing himself up to five cards.

At the end of Gerry’s turn, Lee played a [Scion of Oona](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scion+of+Oona), enhancing his whole team. After untapping, he played [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren). The mighty blue Planeswalker immediately became a little less loyal to draw Lee a card. He also attacked Gerry with his [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique). Gerry chose to chump block with a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), setting a potential [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin) up for later.

Gerry attacked all out on the following turn, and Lee blocked with an animated [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault), his [Scion of Oona](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scion+of+Oona), and his [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique). After combat, Gerry evoked a [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher), which would wipe Lee’s board clean. In response to that, Lee played [Consign to Dream](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consign+to+Dream) to return his [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique) to his hand. Gerry seemed a little annoyed by this, but it resolved. Gerry also had the two damage that was going to go to Lee hit Jace instead. When the [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique) came back, Lee tried to get a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) from Gerry, but GT had a [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin) to bring the targeted [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) back for him.

Lee untapped and attacked with his [Puppeteer Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Puppeteer+Clique), knocking Gerry to fifteen. After Gerry untapped, Lee tried to play a [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique) the tap Gerry down. With the [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique)‘s trigger on the stack, Gerry played a [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher) killing the Puppeteer and leaving the Mistbind nothing to champion.

Lee played a [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom) and championed it with a [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique), giving him a blocker for the upcoming attack. The Clique jumped in front of the Thresher, and Gerry added an [Archon of Justice](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Archon+of+Justice) to his team.

Steht 1, Thompson 1

"Wow, one game for all the marbles. Are you nervous," Ant asked? When both players just shook their heads he chose to empathize in their stead. "I’m nervous for you."

Gerry had an evoked [Wispmare](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wispmare) for Lee’s second-turn [Bitterblossom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bitterblossom), which forced him to run a [Spellstutter Sprite](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spellstutter+Sprite) at the end of Gerry’s third turn in preparation for a [Mistbind Clique](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mistbind+Clique). Gerry had a [Broken Ambitions](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Broken+Ambitions) to counter the Clique, though, making the [Spellstutter Sprite](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spellstutter+Sprite) nothing more than a mere annoyance. While all this setting up was going on, Gerry had made himself a [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) and began to beat down.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/74/55/7455827d3ad5640900c83975aaa9656d7897d0dab692fd6837360c53db5fa5a4.jpg)*Gerry Thompson is clearly just happy to be here.*

Lee had a [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) on his fifth turn, to which Gerry replied, "It’s always good." And good it was. Gerry’s hand contained a [Chameleon Colossus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chameleon+Colossus), [Cloudthresher](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cloudthresher), [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter), and [Cryptic Command](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cryptic+Command). Lee thought for a minute or two, ignoring Gerry’s offers of "advice," before deciding on the Colossus. Gerry ran a [Mulldrifter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mulldrifter) out on his next turn, keeping the cards flowing into his hand, while Lee was running down to a mere two.

Gerry’s [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) ran into a [Mutavault](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mutavault) on their next attack; a trade I’m pretty sure Gerry was just fine with. It did give Lee the ability to trade his [Spellstutter Sprite](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spellstutter+Sprite) for the [Kitchen Finks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kitchen+Finks) on the next turn if he so chose. However, when Gerry played a [Makeshift Mannequin](https://web.archive.org/web/20170727040336/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Makeshift+Mannequin) at the end of Lee’s turn to return the Colossus to play, Lee had a new creature to block. His Sprite jumped in the Colossus’ way, leaving the path clear for an all out attack on the following turn. There was nothing Lee could do but extend his hand.

Gerry Thompson defeats Lee Steht 2-1 to become the Grand Prix-Denver champion!
